# Converter quality report: core-real-generated-local (2026-10-17)

1772 results across azw3, mobi. Gap categories and their kinds are defined in `tests/harness/schema.md`; a book can carry several categories.

## Format: azw3

### 1. Summary

886 books were run for AZW3; 878 (99%) produced an output file and 8 did not. Calibre itself converted 834 of 886. 0 of 878 (0%) converted books pass all three parser gates (mobitool, KindleUnpack, Calibre reader). Failures by parser: mobitool 878, kindleunpack 8, calibre_reader 8. 798 books carry at least one actionable gap (a book can carry several kinds): 372 have unported Calibre transform gaps (47%), 12 have input repair gaps (2%), 796 have writer bug gaps (100%); 1822 category findings in total. Verdict: writer bug gaps affect the most books (100%), so the next effort should fix the KF8/MOBI writer defects, starting with `gate-mobitool`. Systemic defect(s) dominate the totals: `gate-mobitool` (790 of 790 converted books). Leaving them out, 420 books still carry an actionable gap: 372 unported Calibre transform (89%), 12 input repair (3%), 181 writer bug (43%); unported Calibre transform gaps dominate once the systemic defect is set aside. 0 of 886 books have no actionable gap and were fully evaluated; 88 fixed-layout books are out of scope and not counted. Invariants not evaluated (books): text 16, images 16, toc 16, links 16, style 16. Not counted above (category findings): 830 informational, 248 out of scope.

### 2. Top gap categories by affected books

| # | category | books | % of books | description | kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `gate-mobitool` | 790 | 89% | libmobi mobitool cannot read our output | writer bug |
| 2 | `style-lost` | 330 | 37% | Style score below 0.5, or the input has CSS rules and the output none | unported Calibre transform |
| 3 | `links-unresolved` | 223 | 25% | Internal links that no longer resolve | unported Calibre transform |
| 4 | `toc-flat` | 161 | 18% | TOC nesting depth reduced | unported Calibre transform |
| 5 | `images-misreferenced` | 127 | 14% | Image references in the text point at the wrong image or at none | writer bug |

Informational, acceptable, harness and out-of-scope categories are not ranked here (`calibre-header-diff`, `images-resized`, `app-clean-error`, `harness-error`, `fixed-layout-unsupported`), and nothing on a fixed-layout book is; they appear in the per-category breakdown and the per-book table. `text-loss` counts as `input repair` on books whose verdict is error/warning, otherwise as `writer bug`.

## Format: mobi

### 1. Summary

886 books were run for MOBI; 878 (99%) produced an output file and 8 did not. Calibre itself converted 833 of 886. 751 of 878 (86%) converted books pass all three parser gates (mobitool, KindleUnpack, Calibre reader). Failures by parser: mobitool 127. 390 books carry at least one actionable gap (a book can carry several kinds): 326 have unported Calibre transform gaps (84%), 14 have input repair gaps (4%), 309 have writer bug gaps (79%); 1236 category findings in total. Verdict: unported Calibre transform gaps affect the most books (84%), so the next effort should port the missing Calibre transforms, starting with `style-lost`. 407 of 886 books have no actionable gap and were fully evaluated; 1 more have no gap found but were not fully evaluated (harness error, clean refusal or missing invariants); 88 fixed-layout books are out of scope and not counted. Invariants not evaluated (books): text 8, images 8, toc 9, links 8, style 8. Not counted above (category findings): 831 informational, 159 out of scope.

### 2. Top gap categories by affected books

| # | category | books | % of books | description | kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `style-lost` | 276 | 31% | Style score below 0.5, or the input has CSS rules and the output none | unported Calibre transform |
| 2 | `images-misreferenced` | 266 | 30% | Image references in the text point at the wrong image or at none | writer bug |
| 3 | `links-unresolved` | 223 | 25% | Internal links that no longer resolve | unported Calibre transform |
| 4 | `toc-flat` | 161 | 18% | TOC nesting depth reduced | unported Calibre transform |
| 5 | `gate-mobitool` | 126 | 14% | libmobi mobitool cannot read our output | writer bug |

Informational, acceptable, harness and out-of-scope categories are not ranked here (`calibre-header-diff`, `images-resized`, `app-clean-error`, `harness-error`, `fixed-layout-unsupported`), and nothing on a fixed-layout book is; they appear in the per-category breakdown and the per-book table. `text-loss` counts as `input repair` on books whose verdict is error/warning, otherwise as `writer bug`.
